- **persistence-audit**: Verify that every promise the product makes to REMEMBER something is still being kept. For each thing a user can save, set, tick, toggle or submit: does a write actually reach storage, is it read back on the next load, and when did that storage last receive a write from the live application? The primary signal is FRESHNESS, not emptiness — a table with healthy rows that stopped being written looks perfect in every other audit. Run after any UI rewrite, framework migration, route reshuffle or "port the old screen to the new shell", and periodically thereafter. Trigger phrases: "did the redesig… ([Manatee-Outlaw/skill-stack](https://github.com/Manatee-Outlaw/skill-stack/tree/HEAD/plugins/skill-engineering/skills/persistence-audit))
- **persistence-backends**: How Kages persists workspaces: in-memory (default), plain Postgres via DATABASE_URL, or Supabase REST.
